Drops from 5 to 3 on a third straight zero-message week. The 2-point fall is small only because there is almost nothing left to lose: the client churned 53 days ago and the score now measures collectability, not relationship health. What moved it down is that every specific recommendation from the 8/21 file was ignored — the invoice was not issued (44 days past cadence), no off-Telegram escalation was attempted, and the one cheap action available (read the 7/6 screenshot and write the number down) still has not been done. Three weeks of documented ALTR-side inaction on a live receivable is a deterioration, not a plateau. The recommended write-off date of 2026-09-04 is now 7 days out and will arrive with the account in exactly the same state it was in on 8/14.

Third consecutive fully silent week — zero messages from either side, again. The chat's last message of any kind is Stephski's 8/4 nudge, so this thread has now been dead for 24 straight days, and Hannah (Bella) herself has been silent for 53 days since she announced her exit on 7/6. Every action item flagged last week was ignored: the 7/1-7/7 final invoice is still unissued (now 44 days past ALTR's own 7/15 cadence date), the exit balance she volunteered to pay is still uncollected, and nobody has yet opened the 7/6 screenshot (message id 316, captioned "This is total left") that is the only record anywhere of what she owes. A separate and uglier fact surfaced on a full re-read of the history: her churn message was never answered by anyone. Not once. The next two messages in the chat after she quit were Mica's routine reel inspo and Michelle's weekly leak-removal report on 7/10 — business as usual, four days after the client said she was leaving. ALTR is carrying an unquantified receivable against an ex-client on a dead channel, with no owner, and has now spent three weeks doing nothing about it.
